    function shouldShowAnnouncementBar() {
      try {
        if (!window.Static.SQUARESPACE_CONTEXT.showAnnouncementBar) {
          return false;
        }
        var localDataStr = localStorage.getItem('squarespace-announcement-bar');
        if (localDataStr && JSON.parse(localDataStr).closed) {
          return false;
        }
        return true;
      } catch(e) {
        console.error(e);
        return true;
      }
    }
    if (shouldShowAnnouncementBar()) {
      document.body.classList.add('announcement-bar-reserved-space');
    }

      (function() {
        var header = document.getElementById('header');
        if (!header) {
          return;
        }
        function setHeaderHeightVar() {
          document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
            '--header-height',
            header.getBoundingClientRect().height + 'px'
          );
        }
        setHeaderHeightVar();
        var images = header.getElementsByTagName('img');
        for (var i = 0; i < images.length; i++) {
          if (!images[i].complete) {
            images[i].addEventListener('load', setHeaderHeightVar);
          }
        }
      })();
      //# sourceURL=reserveSpaceForHeader.js
    </script>

        $menuWrapper.removeClass("is-hovered");

        const locationLink = $link.attr("data-href").slice(6);
        window.location.href = "/" + locationLink;

      setTimeout(() => {
        const headerHeight = $("#header").height();
        $(".mega-menu-item").css("padding-top", `${headerHeight}px`);
      }, 150);

      mutations.forEach(m => {
        if (m.attributeName === "class") {
          const isShrink = headerEl.classList.contains("shrink");

          if (isShrink !== prevShrink) {
            prevShrink = isShrink;
            updateHeaderHeight();
          }
        }
      });
    });

    observer.observe(headerEl, { attributes: true });
  }
});

<script>
(function () {
  function setupHeaderMenuCtas() {
    const isEditor = window !== window.parent;
    if (isEditor) return;

    const header = document.querySelector("#header");
    if (!header) return;

    const ctaElements = header.querySelectorAll(
      ".header-menu-cta, .header-actions-action--cta"
    );

    ctaElements.forEach((item) => {
      if (item.querySelector(":scope > .cust-hd-button")) return;

      const link = document.createElement("a");
      link.className = "cust-hd-button";
      link.href = "/job-opportunities";
      link.textContent = "Job Opportunities";

      item.prepend(link);
    });

    const rootFolder = header.querySelector(
      '.header-menu-nav-folder[data-folder="root"]'
    );

    const originalCta = rootFolder?.querySelector(".header-menu-cta");
    if (!originalCta) return;

    const folders = header.querySelectorAll(
      '.header-menu-nav-folder:not([data-folder="root"])'
    );

    folders.forEach((folder) => {
      const content = folder.querySelector(".header-menu-nav-folder-content");
      if (!content) return;

      if (content.querySelector(":scope > .header-menu-cta")) return;

      const ctaClone = originalCta.cloneNode(true);

      content.appendChild(ctaClone);
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", setupHeaderMenuCtas);
  window.addEventListener("load", setupHeaderMenuCtas);
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  if (window !== window.parent) return;

  function setTopMargSectionBg() {
    const header = document.querySelector('#header');
    if (!header) return;

    const headerHeight = header.offsetHeight;

    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('section[id*="topmarg"]');

    sections.forEach(section => {
      const sectionBg = section.querySelector('.section-background');
      if (!sectionBg) return;

      sectionBg.style.marginTop = `${headerHeight}px`;
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', setTopMargSectionBg);
  window.addEventListener('load', setTopMargSectionBg);
})();
</script>


<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const sections = document.querySelectorAll("#features-slider");

  const fields = {
    title: {
      selector: ".list-item-content__title",
      className: "custom-feature-title",
      tag: "div",
    },
    image: {
      selector: ".list-item-media",
      className: "custom-feature-image",
      tag: "div",
    },
    description: {
      selector: ".list-item-content__description",
      className: "custom-feature-description",
      tag: "div",
    },
  };

  function createElementFromField(item, field) {
    const originalElement = item.querySelector(field.selector);
    if (!originalElement) return "";

    return `
      <${field.tag} class="${field.className}">
        ${originalElement.innerHTML}
      </${field.tag}>
    `;
  }

  function createSlideTemplate(item) {
    const title = createElementFromField(item, fields.title);
    const image = createElementFromField(item, fields.image);
    const description = createElementFromField(item, fields.description);

    return `
      <div class="custom-feature-slide swiper-slide">
		<div class="before-ic"></div>
        <div class="item-main-wrapper">
          ${title}
          ${image}
          ${description}
		</div>
		<div class="after-ic"></div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  sections.forEach((section) => {
    const originalList = section.querySelector(".user-items-list-item-container");
    const originalItems = section.querySelectorAll(".user-items-list-item-container .list-item");

    if (!originalList || !originalItems.length) return;
    if (section.querySelector(".custom-features-swiper")) return;

    const swiperEl = document.createElement("div");
    swiperEl.className = "custom-features-swiper swiper";

    const swiperWrapper = document.createElement("div");
    swiperWrapper.className = "swiper-wrapper";

    originalItems.forEach((item) => {
      swiperWrapper.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", createSlideTemplate(item));
    });

    const pagination = document.createElement("div");
    pagination.className = "swiper-pagination";

    swiperEl.appendChild(swiperWrapper);
    swiperEl.appendChild(pagination);

    originalList.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", swiperEl);

    new Swiper(swiperEl, {
      slidesPerView: 1.2,
      spaceBetween: 8,
      grabCursor: true,

      breakpoints: {
        768: {
          slidesPerView: 3,
          spaceBetween: 24,
          grabCursor: false,
          enabled: false,
        }
      },

      pagination: {
        el: pagination,
        clickable: true,
      },
      
    });
  });
});
</script>


<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

  const isEditor = window.self !== window.top;
  if (isEditor) return;

  const sections = document.querySelectorAll("#testimonials-slider-v2");

  function splitTitleByPipe(text) {
    const parts = text
      .split("|")
      .map(function (part) {
        return part.trim();
      })
      .filter(Boolean);

    if (!parts.length) return "";

    return parts
      .map(function (part, index) {
        return `
          <span class="custom-testimonial-title-part custom-testimonial-title-part-${index + 1}">
            ${part}
          </span>
        `;
      })
      .join("");
  }

  function createImage(item) {
    const image = item.querySelector(".list-item-media");
    if (!image) return "";

    return `
      <div class="custom-testimonial-image">
        ${image.innerHTML}
      </div>
    `;
  }

  function createTitle(item) {
    const titleEl = item.querySelector(".list-item-content__title");
    if (!titleEl) return "";

    const titleHTML = splitTitleByPipe(titleEl.textContent);

    if (!titleHTML) return "";

    return `
      <div class="custom-testimonial-title">
        ${titleHTML}
      </div>
    `;
  }

  function createDescriptionParts(item) {
    const description = item.querySelector(".list-item-content__description");
    const button = item.querySelector(".list-item-content__button-wrapper");

    const result = {
      label: "",
      quote: "",
      source: "",
      button: button ? button.innerHTML : ""
    };

    if (!description) return result;

    const paragraphs = description.querySelectorAll("p");

    paragraphs.forEach(function (p) {
      const hasEm = p.querySelector("em");
      const hasStrong = p.querySelector("strong");
      const hasUnderline = p.querySelector("u");

      if (hasEm) {
        result.label += p.textContent.trim();
        return;
      }

      if (hasStrong) {
        result.quote += `
          <p>${p.textContent.trim()}</p>
        `;
        return;
      }

      if (hasUnderline) {
        result.source += p.textContent.trim();
      }
    });

    return result;
  }

  function createSlideTemplate(item) {
    const image = createImage(item);
    const title = createTitle(item);
    const desc = createDescriptionParts(item);

    return `
      <div class="custom-testimonial-slide swiper-slide">

        ${
          desc.label
            ? `
              <div class="custom-testimonial-label">
                <span>${desc.label}</span>
              </div>
            `
            : ""
        }

        ${
          desc.quote
            ? `
              <div class="custom-testimonial-quote">
                ${desc.quote}
              </div>
            `
            : ""
        }

        ${
          image || title
            ? `
              <div class="custom-testimonial-author">
                ${image}
                ${title}
              </div>
            `
            : ""
        }

        ${
          desc.source || desc.button
            ? `
              <div class="custom-testimonial-bottom">
                ${
                  desc.source
                    ? `<div class="custom-testimonial-source">${desc.source}</div>`
                    : ""
                }

                ${
                  desc.button
                    ? `
                      <div class="custom-testimonial-button">
                        ${desc.button}
                      </div>
                    `
                    : ""
                }
              </div>
            `
            : ""
        }

      </div>
    `;
  }

  function createControlsTemplate() {
    return `
      <div class="custom-testimonial-controls">
        <div class="custom-testimonial-controls-button">
          <a href="/testimonials" class="sqs-button-element--primary">See All Testimonials</a>
        </div>

        <div class="custom-testimonial-pagination"></div>

        <div class="custom-testimonial-arrows">
          <button 
            class="custom-testimonial-arrow custom-testimonial-arrow-prev" 
            type="button" 
            aria-label="Previous slide">
          </button>

          <button 
            class="custom-testimonial-arrow custom-testimonial-arrow-next" 
            type="button" 
            aria-label="Next slide">
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  function setSwiperLeftOffset(section) {
    const originalList = section.querySelector(".user-items-list-item-container");
    const swiperEl = section.querySelector(".custom-testimonial-swiper");

    if (!originalList || !swiperEl) return;

    const styles = window.getComputedStyle(originalList);

    const paddingLeft = parseFloat(styles.paddingLeft) || 0;
    const marginLeft = parseFloat(styles.marginLeft) || 0;

    const totalLeftOffset = paddingLeft + marginLeft;

    swiperEl.style.marginLeft = totalLeftOffset + "px";
  }

  sections.forEach(function (section) {
    const originalList = section.querySelector(".user-items-list-item-container");
    const originalItems = section.querySelectorAll(".user-items-list-item-container .list-item");

    if (!originalList || !originalItems.length) return;
    if (section.querySelector(".custom-testimonial-swiper")) return;

    const swiperEl = document.createElement("div");
    swiperEl.className = "custom-testimonial-swiper swiper";

    const swiperWrapper = document.createElement("div");
    swiperWrapper.className = "swiper-wrapper";

    originalItems.forEach(function (item) {
      swiperWrapper.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", createSlideTemplate(item));
    });

    swiperEl.appendChild(swiperWrapper);

    const controlsWrapper = document.createElement("div");
    controlsWrapper.innerHTML = createControlsTemplate();

    const controlsEl = controlsWrapper.firstElementChild;

    originalList.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", swiperEl);
    swiperEl.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", controlsEl);

    setSwiperLeftOffset(section);

    const swiperInstance = new Swiper(swiperEl, {
      slidesPerView: 1.1,
      spaceBetween: 14,
      grabCursor: true,
      loop: false,
      slidesOffsetAfter: 20,

      breakpoints: {
        620: {
          slidesPerView: 2.1,
          spaceBetween: 24
        }
      },

      pagination: {
        el: controlsEl.querySelector(".custom-testimonial-pagination"),
        clickable: true
      },

      navigation: {
        nextEl: controlsEl.querySelector(".custom-testimonial-arrow-next"),
        prevEl: controlsEl.querySelector(".custom-testimonial-arrow-prev")
      }
    });

    window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
      setSwiperLeftOffset(section);
      swiperInstance.update();
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const sections = document.querySelectorAll("#results-slider");

  function padNumber(num) {
    return String(num).padStart(2, "0");
  }

  function splitTitle(titleText) {
    const slashIndex = titleText.indexOf("/");

    if (slashIndex === -1) {
      return titleText;
    }

    const before = titleText.slice(0, slashIndex).trim();
    const after = titleText.slice(slashIndex).trim();

    return `<span class="cust-color">${before}</span> ${after}`;
  }

  function createTitle(item, index, total) {
    const titleEl = item.querySelector(".list-item-content__title");
    if (!titleEl) return "";

    return `
      <div class="custom-results-top">
        <div class="custom-results-title">
          ${splitTitle(titleEl.textContent.trim())}
        </div>

        <div class="custom-results-count">
          ${padNumber(index + 1)}/${padNumber(total)}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  function createStatBlock(thirdP) {
    if (!thirdP) return "";

    const number = thirdP.querySelector("strong")?.textContent.trim() || "";
    const unit = thirdP.querySelector("em")?.textContent.trim() || "";

    const temp = thirdP.cloneNode(true);

    temp.querySelectorAll("strong, em, u").forEach((el) => {
      el.replaceWith(el.textContent);
    });

    let text = temp.textContent.trim();

    if (number) text = text.replace(number, "");
    if (unit) text = text.replace(unit, "");

    text = text.trim();

    return `
      <div class="custom-results-stat">
        ${number ? `<span class="custom-results-number">${number}</span>` : ""}
        ${unit ? `<span class="custom-results-unit">${unit}</span>` : ""}
        ${text ? `<span class="custom-results-text">${text}</span>` : ""}
      </div>
    `;
  }

  function createDescriptionParts(item) {
    const description = item.querySelector(".list-item-content__description");
    const button = item.querySelector(".list-item-content__button-wrapper");

    const paragraphs = description ? description.querySelectorAll("p") : [];

    const firstP = paragraphs[0] || null;
    const secondP = paragraphs[1] || null;
    const thirdP = paragraphs[2] || null;
    const fourthP = paragraphs[3] || null;
    const lastP = paragraphs[4] || null;

    return {
      category: firstP
        ? `
          <div class="custom-results-category">
            <span class="cust-color">${firstP.textContent.trim()}</span>
          </div>
        `
        : "",

      name: secondP
        ? `
          <div class="custom-results-name">
            <span class="custom-results-name-inner">${secondP.textContent.trim()}</span>
          </div>
        `
        : "",

      stat: createStatBlock(thirdP),

      statText: fourthP
        ? `
          <div class="custom-results-stat-text">
            ${fourthP.textContent.trim()}
          </div>
        `
        : "",

      bottom: lastP || button
        ? `
          <div class="custom-results-bottom">
            ${
              lastP
                ? `<div class="custom-results-bottom-text">${lastP.textContent.trim()}</div>`
                : ""
            }

            ${
              button
                ? `<div class="custom-results-button">${button.innerHTML}</div>`
                : ""
            }
          </div>
        `
        : "",
    };
  }

  function createSlideTemplate(item, index, total) {
    const title = createTitle(item, index, total);
    const desc = createDescriptionParts(item);

    return `
      <div class="custom-results-slide swiper-slide">
        ${title}

        <div class="custom-results-content">
          ${desc.category}
          ${desc.name}
          ${desc.stat}
          ${desc.statText}
        </div>

        ${desc.bottom}
      </div>
    `;
  }

  sections.forEach((section) => {
    const originalList = section.querySelector(".user-items-list-item-container");
    const originalItems = section.querySelectorAll(".user-items-list-item-container .list-item");

    if (!originalList || !originalItems.length) return;
    if (section.querySelector(".custom-results-swiper")) return;

    const swiperEl = document.createElement("div");
    swiperEl.className = "custom-results-swiper swiper";

    const swiperWrapper = document.createElement("div");
    swiperWrapper.className = "swiper-wrapper";

    originalItems.forEach((item, index) => {
      swiperWrapper.insertAdjacentHTML(
        "beforeend",
        createSlideTemplate(item, index, originalItems.length)
      );
    });

    const pagination = document.createElement("div");
    pagination.className = "custom-results-pagination swiper-pagination";

    swiperEl.appendChild(swiperWrapper);
    swiperEl.appendChild(pagination);

    originalList.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", swiperEl);

    new Swiper(swiperEl, {
      slidesPerView: 1.2,
      spaceBetween: 4,
      grabCursor: true,
      loop: false,
centeredSlides: true,
      initialSlide: 1,

      breakpoints: {
        920: {
          slidesPerView: 2.4,
          spaceBetween: 4,
        },
      },

      navigation: {
        nextEl: ".results-button-next",
        prevEl: ".results-button-prev",
      },

      pagination: {
        el: pagination,
        clickable: true,
      },
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  function isEditor() {
    return window !== window.parent;
  }

  function reinitSquarespace(context) {
    const root = context || document;

    // 1. Standard Squarespace layout/block reinit
    if (
      window.Squarespace &&
      typeof window.Squarespace.initializeLayoutBlocks === 'function'
    ) {
      try {
        window.Squarespace.initializeLayoutBlocks(root);
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('initializeLayoutBlocks error:', e);
      }
    }

    // 2. Same lifecycle idea as lightbox plugins use
    if (
      window.Squarespace &&
      typeof window.Squarespace.afterBodyLoad === 'function'
    ) {
      try {
        window.Squarespace.AFTER_BODY_LOADED = false;
        window.Squarespace.afterBodyLoad();
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('afterBodyLoad error:', e);
      }
    }

    // 3. Refresh images / fluid layouts
    try {
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'));
    } catch (e) {}

    setTimeout(function () {
      try {
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'));
      } catch (e) {}
    }, 300);
  }

  function initCustomTabs() {
    if (isEditor()) return;

    const tabsSection = document.querySelector('#custom-tabs-section');
    const target = document.querySelector('#tabs-content-target');
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#custom-tabs-section .custom-tab-btn');

    if (!tabsSection || !target || !tabs.length) return;

    // Prevent double initialization
    if (target.dataset.tabsInitialized === 'true') return;
    target.dataset.tabsInitialized = 'true';

    const panels = [];

    tabs.forEach(function (tab, index) {
      const tabName = tab.dataset.tab;
      if (!tabName) return;

      const sourceSection = document.querySelector('#tab-section-' + tabName);
      if (!sourceSection) {
        console.warn('Tab section not found: #tab-section-' + tabName);
        return;
      }

      sourceSection.classList.add('custom-tab-panel');
      sourceSection.dataset.tabPanel = tabName;

      if (index === 0) {
        sourceSection.classList.add('active');
        tab.classList.add('active');
      } else {
        sourceSection.classList.remove('active');
        tab.classList.remove('active');
      }

      // Important: move original DOM node, do not clone and do not copy innerHTML
      target.appendChild(sourceSection);
      panels.push(sourceSection);
    });

    // Reinit once after moving all sections with forms
    setTimeout(function () {
      reinitSquarespace(target);
    }, 100);

    tabs.forEach(function (tab) {
      tab.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();

        const tabName = tab.dataset.tab;
        if (!tabName) return;

        tabs.forEach(function (item) {
          item.classList.remove('active');
        });

        panels.forEach(function (panel) {
          panel.classList.toggle('active', panel.dataset.tabPanel === tabName);
        });

        tab.classList.add('active');

        // Do not fully reinit forms on every click.
        // Resize is enough for fluid-engine/grid recalculation.
        try {
          window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'));
        } catch (e) {}

        setTimeout(function () {
          try {
            window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'));
          } catch (e) {}
        }, 150);
      });
    });
  }

  window.addEventListener('load', initCustomTabs);

  // For Squarespace AJAX navigation
  document.addEventListener('mercury:load', initCustomTabs);
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initCustomTabs);
})();
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const mobileQuery = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)");

  const sourceBlockIds = [
    "block-2c6731bd092a8d0be5b0",
    "block-yui_3_17_2_1_1781092931540_9428",
    "block-ec9e1da3c87492c6f0a2",
    "block-fc85d5600a1c7be0f9b2",
  ];

  const targetWrapper = document.querySelector(".custom-footer-navigation");
  if (!targetWrapper) return;

  function buildFooterNavigation() {
    if (!mobileQuery.matches) return;
    if (targetWrapper.dataset.footerNavReady === "true") return;

    targetWrapper.innerHTML = "";

    sourceBlockIds.forEach((id) => {
      const sourceBlock = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!sourceBlock) return;

      const sourceContent = sourceBlock.querySelector(".sqs-html-content");
      if (!sourceContent) return;

      const paragraphs = sourceContent.querySelectorAll(":scope > p");
      if (!paragraphs.length) return;

      const item = document.createElement("div");
      item.className = "custom-footer-nav-item";

      const title = document.createElement("div");
      title.className = "custom-footer-nav-title";

      const content = document.createElement("div");
      content.className = "custom-footer-nav-content";

      paragraphs.forEach((paragraph, index) => {
        const clonedParagraph = paragraph.cloneNode(true);

        if (index === 0) {
          title.appendChild(clonedParagraph);
        } else {
          content.appendChild(clonedParagraph);
        }
      });

      item.appendChild(title);
      item.appendChild(content);
      targetWrapper.appendChild(item);
    });

    targetWrapper
      .querySelectorAll(".custom-footer-nav-title")
      .forEach((title) => {
        title.addEventListener("click", () => {
          const item = title.closest(".custom-footer-nav-item");
          if (!item) return;

          item.classList.toggle("is-open");
        });
      });

    targetWrapper.dataset.footerNavReady = "true";
  }

  function resetFooterNavigation() {
    if (mobileQuery.matches) return;

    targetWrapper.innerHTML = "";
    targetWrapper.dataset.footerNavReady = "false";
  }

  buildFooterNavigation();

  mobileQuery.addEventListener("change", () => {
    resetFooterNavigation();
    buildFooterNavigation();
  });
});
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const isEditor = window !== window.parent;
  if (isEditor) return;

  const sections = [
    ...document.querySelectorAll("section[id^='slider-get-started']")
  ];

  if (!sections.length) return;

  let wrapper = document.querySelector(".custom-get-started-wrapper");

  if (!wrapper) {
    wrapper = document.createElement("div");
    wrapper.className = "custom-get-started-wrapper";

    sections[0].parentNode.insertBefore(wrapper, sections[0]);

    sections.forEach((section) => {
      wrapper.appendChild(section);
    });
  }

  const listSection = wrapper.querySelector(
    "section[id^='slider-get-started']:not([id^='slider-get-started-top']):not([id^='slider-get-started-bottom'])"
  );

  if (!listSection) return;

  const bgImage = listSection.querySelector(".section-background img");

  if (bgImage) {
    const bgUrl =
      bgImage.getAttribute("src") ||
      bgImage.getAttribute("data-src") ||
      bgImage.getAttribute("data-image");

    if (bgUrl) {
      wrapper.style.backgroundImage = `url("${bgUrl}")`;
    }
  }

  const list = listSection.querySelector(".user-items-list-item-container");
  if (!list) return;

  if (listSection.querySelector(".custom-get-started-slider")) return;

  const items = [...list.querySelectorAll(":scope > .list-item")];
  if (!items.length) return;

  const slider = document.createElement("div");
  slider.className = "custom-get-started-slider swiper";

  const swiperWrapper = document.createElement("div");
  swiperWrapper.className = "swiper-wrapper";

  items.forEach((item, index) => {
    const image = item.querySelector(".list-item-media img");
    const title = item.querySelector(".list-item-content__title");
    const description = item.querySelector(".list-item-content__description");

    const slide = document.createElement("div");
    slide.className = "custom-get-started-slide swiper-slide";

    const step = document.createElement("div");
    step.className = "custom-get-started-step";

    const stepText = document.createElement("span");
    stepText.className = "custom-get-started-step-text";
    stepText.textContent = "Step";

    const stepNumber = document.createElement("span");
    stepNumber.className = "custom-get-started-step-number";
    stepNumber.textContent = String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0");

    step.appendChild(stepText);
    step.appendChild(stepNumber);

    const media = document.createElement("div");
    media.className = "custom-get-started-media";

    if (image) {
      const newImage = image.cloneNode(true);
      newImage.removeAttribute("style");
      newImage.className = "custom-get-started-image";
      media.appendChild(newImage);
    }

    const content = document.createElement("div");
    content.className = "custom-get-started-content";

    if (title) {
      const newTitle = document.createElement("h3");
      newTitle.className = "custom-get-started-title";
      newTitle.textContent = title.textContent.trim();
      content.appendChild(newTitle);
    }

    if (description) {
      const newDescription = document.createElement("div");
      newDescription.className = "custom-get-started-description";
      newDescription.innerHTML = description.innerHTML;
      content.appendChild(newDescription);
    }

    slide.appendChild(step);
    slide.appendChild(media);
    slide.appendChild(content);

    swiperWrapper.appendChild(slide);
  });

  slider.appendChild(swiperWrapper);

  const listWrapper = list.closest(".user-items-list");

  if (listWrapper) {
    listWrapper.appendChild(slider);
  } else {
    list.after(slider);
  }

  if (typeof Swiper === "undefined") return;

  new Swiper(slider, {
    slidesPerView: 1.1,
    spaceBetween: 12,
    grabCursor: true,
    watchOverflow: true,
    breakpoints: {
      768: {
        slidesPerView: 3,
        spaceBetween: 24
      }
    }
  });
});
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const section = document.querySelector("#testimonials-list");
  if (!section) return;

  const items = section.querySelectorAll(".list-item");

  items.forEach((item) => {
    const title = item.querySelector(".list-item-content__title");
    const media = item.querySelector(".list-item-media");

    if (!title || !media) return;

    if (title.dataset.titleProcessed === "true") return;

    const originalText = title.textContent.trim();

    if (originalText.includes("|")) {
      const parts = originalText.split("|");
      const mainTitle = parts[0].trim();
      const subtitle = parts.slice(1).join("|").trim();

      title.innerHTML = "";
      title.append(document.createTextNode(mainTitle));

      if (subtitle) {
        const span = document.createElement("span");
        span.className = "list-item-title-meta";
        span.textContent = subtitle;
        title.appendChild(span);
      }
    }

    title.dataset.titleProcessed = "true";
    media.appendChild(title);
  });
});
